# Phase 2 Action 2.4 - Data, Retrieval, Memory, and Prompt Evidence

## Observation class

**SOURCE-OBSERVED STATIC CANDIDATES ONLY. RUNTIME PRESENCE, DATA FLOW, ACCESS CONTROL, AND ISOLATION ARE UNVERIFIED.**

## Scope

This action inventories static source candidates related to data, datasets, documents, indexes, embeddings, vector stores, memory, caches, prompts, and templates.

No runtime service was started and no external service was contacted.

## Method

- Enumerated tracked files using `git ls-files`.
- Examined text files up to 2 MiB.
- Skipped binary files.
- Excluded `docs/security/` from candidate discovery.
- Stored file paths and match counts rather than source contents or data values.
- Keyword matches are discovery candidates and are not proof of runtime data flow or security enforcement.

## Scan summary

- Tracked text files scanned: **7451**
- Large files skipped: **1**
- Binary files skipped: **194**

## Security interpretation

The inventory identifies source locations for deeper reverse engineering. It does not establish actual runtime data flows, document ownership, retrieval authorization, vector isolation, memory isolation, cache isolation, prompt provenance, or template safety.

Later controlled actions must establish trust boundaries, data lineage, authorization behavior, tenant isolation, retrieval behavior, lifecycle behavior, and failure paths.
